# Skills · A · part 41

834 entries, adduct-mz-variant-prediction to adinsights-release-readiness. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **adduct-mz-variant-prediction**: Use when a neutral-mass compound database (e.g. HMDB, FooDB) must be converted into an m/z-searchable annotation resource, or when matching observed peak m/z values (with a known ppm error margin) against compounds whose adduct and isotope variants are not yet enumerated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-mz-variant-prediction))
- **adduct-notation-normalization**: Use when merging spectral-library or feature-table records whose ion-identity annotations appear in inconsistent spellings (e.g. 'M + H', 'M + H +', '[M + H]+') and you need them aggregated as one ion identity before networking or counting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-notation-normalization))
- **adduct-precursor-mass-calculation**: Use when a compound list supplies molecular formulas or SMILES strings (target/suspect entries) and precursor ion masses must be derived under a chosen adduct before peak extraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-precursor-mass-calculation))
- **adduct-probability-feature-correlation-scoring**: Use when an untargeted metabolomics dataset carries multiple putative annotations per feature (CMM-generated annotation tables) and annotations must be prioritized for pathway analysis using adduct probability and feature-correlation evidence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-probability-feature-correlation-scoring))
- **adduct-probability-ranking**: Use when a RowMerger-merged annotation table contains multiple putative annotations per feature and a feature-intensity table is available, and the goal is to prioritize the most probable annotation per feature. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-probability-ranking))
- **adduct-redundancy-accounting**: Use when counting unique lipid ions, features, or lipid types from annotation workbooks produced by LipidMatch or comparable lipid identification software, where multiple adducts of the same lipid species appear across features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-redundancy-accounting))
- **adduct-reference-mass-matching**: Use when you have a binned mass-difference histogram from MALDI-MSI peak pairs and want to count occurrences of known adducts (Na+, K+, 13C, DHB or CHCA matrix adducts) within instrument mass tolerance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-reference-mass-matching))
- **adduct-regex-pattern-matching**: Use when ingesting mass spectrometry spectra from heterogeneous databases or libraries where adduct annotations may be incomplete, incorrectly formatted, or inconsistent with the ionization mode. Use it before downstream analysis (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-regex-pattern-matching) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-relationship-inference**: Use when a processed, annotated LC-MS feature table (tidymass massdataset) contains suspected in-source fragmentation products misannotated as genuine metabolites and no in-pipeline deconvolution exists. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-relationship-inference))
- **adduct-rule-based-ion-annotation**: Use when you have CAMERA pseudo-spectra from LC/MS data in positive or negative ion mode and need to label peaks as isotope, adduct or fragment species and derive the quasi-molecular ion mass. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-rule-based-ion-annotation))
- **adduct-rule-table-matching**: Use when grouping has produced CAMERA compound spectra from LC/MS peak lists (positive and/or negative ESI mode) and you need to assign ion-species annotations and propose accurate compound masses from adduct/isotope rules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-rule-table-matching))
- **adduct-series-mass-difference-validation**: Use when identify_adducts() output from a direct-injection plasma ionization (DBDI) MS dataset must be checked: candidate features should share a base analyte and differ by exact O-adduct deltas. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-series-mass-difference-validation))
- **adduct-signature-identification-mass-spectrometry**: Use when you have statistically significant LC-MS features (e.g., filtered by p-value &lt; 0.01) from multi-assay metabolomics datasets and need to group features that represent the same metabolite ionized under different ESI conditions (e.g., [M+H]+, [M+Na]+, [M+NH4]+). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-signature-identification-mass-spectrometry) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-species-annotation**: Use when you have an untargeted LC-MS metabolomics feature table (m/z per sample) plus MS2 spectra in .mgf or .mzML format and need to assign metabolite identities to peaks at MS1 and MS2 levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-species-annotation))
- **adduct-species-simulation**: Use when you need to stress-test an LC-HRMS feature alignment tool (e.g. MetMatch) against a known ground truth by injecting artificial adduct variants and retention-time perturbations into a reference feature list. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-species-simulation))
- **adduct-specific-ccs-benchmarking**: Use when you have a dataset of compounds with SMILES, an adduct type from {[M+H]+, [M-H]-, [M+Na]+}, and experimental CCS values, and need to quantitatively compare two or more CCS predictors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-specific-ccs-benchmarking))
- **adduct-specific-model-comparison**: Use when you have trained CCSP 2.0 linear SVR models for multiple adduct ion types and need to understand which Mordred descriptor classes and counts each adduct-specific model relies on. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-specific-model-comparison))
- **adduct-specific-model-fine-tuning**: Use when when you have access to annotated MS/MS spectra from a specific ionization mode (e.g., negative ESI) or adduct class (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-specific-model-fine-tuning) · 3 copies)
- **adduct-state-matching-for-ccs-comparison**: Use when experimentally measured CCS values (e.g., TWIMS from Supporting Information Tables S1/S2) must be compared against literature CCS values per metabolite/adduct pair to quantify between-laboratory deviation or justify a ±3% acceptance threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-state-matching-for-ccs-comparison))
- **adduct-stratified-model-evaluation**: Use when you need to rank a machine-learning collision cross section predictor (e.g. CCSP 2.0) against alternatives like CCSbase, AllCCS or DeepCCS using held-out test splits drawn from a measured CCS compendium stratified by adduct type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-stratified-model-evaluation))
- **adduct-type-assignment-positive-mode**: Use when you have an unknown MS/MS spectrum with a measured precursor m/z and want to determine which positive-mode adduct type ([M+H]+, [M+Na]+, [M+K]+, etc.) is most likely responsible for ionization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-type-assignment-positive-mode) · 2 copies)
